- **lpi**: Drive the pi learning system's gated update and end-to-end testing from opencode. Use when the user asks to update pi or its extension packages, run lpi check/update/doctor/rollback, e2e the learning system, test whether an update or code change works in the learner sandbox, or triage a failed lpi update / diagnosis report and make lpi test pass. ([delightaheebwa/learning-pi](https://github.com/delightaheebwa/learning-pi/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/opencode-lpi-skill))
- **lpi-add-delta**: Add a single delta — a new report, dashboard, form, flow, permit/license type, field, or small feature — to an ALREADY-generated LPI engagement without re-running lpi-generate-implementation. Use when the user says 'add a report to a client', 'add a new form', 'add a permit type to the existing build', 'new dashboard for a client', 'ship this change to a client without regenerating', or 'incremental deploy'. Authors the delta in the correct tier, emits a scoped additive package.xml, and records it in the engagement delta-log. Never regenerates, never re-runs seeds. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-3-expand))
- **lr-4-intake**: Stage 4 of the related-work pipeline. Defines the synthesis set (every paper read in full at priority 1–2), matches each to its library/refs.bib entry and PDF, files PDFs as library/pdfs/&lt;citekey&gt;.pdf, records citekeys, closes out every other candidate, and reports anything missing or mismatched. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-4-intake))
- **lr-5-synthesize**: Stage 5 of the related-work pipeline, run after intake and before notes. Over every paper read in full, learns how the field organizes itself from the papers' own related-work sections, writes claim-free summaries guided by that, builds a comparison table, places our paper in it, revisits the categories, writes a paragraph plan to notes/synthesis.md, and chooses the papers the section cites. Fully automatic; a fresh-context reviewer replaces human review. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-5-synthesize))
- **lr-6-notes**: Stage 6 of the related-work pipeline. Writes one evidence note per paper the synthesis chose to cite, with note-writer subagents, and checks each note against what the synthesis plans to say about that paper. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-6-notes))
- **lr-7-draft**: Stage 7 of the related-work pipeline. Drafts writing/related_work.tex from the synthesis (notes/synthesis.md) and the per-paper notes and summaries, citing only keys in library/refs.bib. Run in a fresh session. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-7-draft))
- **lr-8-humanize**: Stage 8 of the related-work pipeline. Runs the humanizer skill on writing/related_work.tex to remove AI writing patterns, then verifies that every citation key and the LaTeX survived unchanged. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-8-humanize))
- **lr-9-check**: Stage 9 of the related-work pipeline. Checks every sentence that cites a paper against that paper's PDF and note with one citation-checker subagent per citekey, applies minimal fixes, and writes checks/report.md. ([taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills](https://github.com/taylorsm9/related_work_claude_skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lr-9-check))

- **LSC 01 — Business Objective and Context Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Business Objective and Context" canvas with the sponsor — stakeholders, success goals, business objective, ambition level, strategic purpose, social/environmental impact, risks and restrictions — so every later canvas has a funded, measurable why. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-business-objective-context))
- **LSC 02 — Immersion Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Immersion" canvas — best-guess customer problems, how customers cope today, inspiring analogues, hottest start-ups, the data we have, what it tells us, and the new data we need and how to get it — before any interview or ideation. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-immersion))
- **LSC 03 — Customer Groups Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Customer Groups" canvas — up to three named customer groups, each with a description and an assumed need worth solving, one marked as the main group, plus the needs common to all segments. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-customer-groups))
- **LSC 04 — Script Creator (Insight Interview Preparation)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Script Creator" canvas — what to learn about the interviewee, a guessed journey timeline with actions, motives, emotions and values per phase, intro, thank-you and documentation plan — and turn it into a tested interview script. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-script-creator))
- **LSC 05 — Insight Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Insight" canvas — per interviewee needs and key findings, what they think and feel, what surprised us — then select the needs worth meeting for the business objective as "the user needs a way to / it's important because / related emotions and values". ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-insight))
- **LSC 06 — Ideation Sandbox Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Ideation Sandbox" — frame the sandbox with the user need, emotions and values, purpose for the user, business objective, company and team mission and purpose for society, then generate ideas that fill need, objective and/or society and choose a seed idea. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-ideation-sandbox))
- **LSC 07 — Idea Accelerator Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Idea Accelerator" — take the seed idea and generate three best improvements each for user-need fit, the behavioural gap, the business objective and social purpose, write the accelerated holistic concept, and decide whether to loop with it as the new seed. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-idea-accelerator))
- **LSC 08 — Rational Concept Sheet**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Rational Concept Sheet" — concept name, how it works, value to the end user and to our business, differentiation, social and environmental impact, and the rational value proposition as END USER / NEED / SOLUTION. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-rational-concept-sheet))
- **LSC 09 — Concept Sheet (Emotional Value Proposition & Fake Ad)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Concept Sheet" — negative and positive emotions, the value gap, how the concept answers each, the emotional value proposition, the combined rational-plus-emotional proposition, the mental reward, and a fake advertisement as the first prototype. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-emotional-concept-sheet))
- **LSC 10 — Impact Optimizer Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Impact Optimizer" — list the concept's positive and negative social and environmental impacts, tweak the concept to raise the good and cut the bad, and decide whether the net impact on the world is positive. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-impact-optimizer))
- **LSC 11 — Customer Engagement Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Customer Engagement" canvas — walk the six stages from awareness to advocacy around the value proposition, name what prevents and what enables each transition, and derive key activities, resources and partners. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-customer-engagement))
- **LSC 12 — Business Model & Market Size Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Business Model & Market Size" canvas — who pays whom and how much, revenue model, today's payments and price anchoring, total addressable and potential target market, first-year and first-month sales, cost structure and key EBIT elements — as a high-level business case with ranges and evidence tags. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-business-model-market-size))
- **LSC 13 — Feasibility Study Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Feasibility Study" — find the biggest implementation issues, define how to validate each and record findings, look ahead with "Back to the future", and cut project risk by simplifying the service while checking the effect on the value proposition. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-feasibility-study))
- **LSC 14 — Evaluating the Concept (The Acid Test)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Evaluating the Concept" radar — score twelve criteria from societal impact to financing with evidence tags, compare concepts, and give a gate verdict of NO, WE HAVE TO ITERATE or YES with the canvases to revisit. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-concept-evaluation))
- **LSC 15 — Validation Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Validation" canvas — define how to validate the value proposition, the customer group and the business model, record results and conclusions (segment size, price in euros), plot each on an evidence-versus-direction chart, and give a YES / ITERATE / NO verdict. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-validation))
- **LSC 16 — Minimum Lovable Product Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Minimum Lovable Product" canvas — the absolute minimum user needs, business requirements and implementation, ranked Must / Should / Could, with everything else parked in the MVP backlog. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-minimum-lovable-product))
- **LSC 17 — Pitch Creator Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Pitch Creator" — wrap the work into challenge-as-opportunity, why, how, validated target group and needs, what, validation, business/user/social impact and next steps, so that others commit; ends with an evidence-gated one-page pitch. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-pitch-creator))
- **LSC 18 — Weekly :) Team Capacity & Wellbeing Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Weekly :)" canvas — a four-week view per team member with holidays, used to plan capacity and keep the team happy and motivated through the build phase. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-team-weekly))
- **LSC 19 — Weekly LSC Board**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Weekly LSC Board" — the weekly rhythm for the build phase: most critical technical, business and end-user issue, the week's biggest fear, what the work validates, last and next retro / user test / demo / shared goals, and a stakeholder-visibility score trend. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-weekly-board))
- **LSC 20 — Agile Board Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Agile Board" — stories flowing across To do, In progress, Testing and Done with the team's own definition of done, seeded from the Minimum Lovable Product and linked to validation hypotheses. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-agile-board))
- **LSC 21 — Experimenting Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Experimenting" canvas — list the main assumptions, label each "think / know / checked", design the experiment and success criteria before running, record key findings, and name what will make the concept fail. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-experimenting))
- **LSC 22 — Retrospective Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Retrospective" — keep doing, do more/start, do less/stop, then choose one to four changes with owners, and open each retro by reviewing the previous one's results. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-retrospective))
- **LSC 23 — Retro Diamond Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Retro Diamond" — a running record of the team changes that worked, written as reusable learnings and shared with other teams and into the vault. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-retro-diamond))
- **LSC 24 — Trends Canvas (Management Track)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Trends" canvas — pick the megatrends that matter, see how each shows up in daily news and in advertising, media, entertainment and products, then derive the trends and phenomena that bring opportunities or threats to the project. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-trends))
- **LSC 25 — Strategic Business Objectives Canvas (Management Track)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Strategic Business Objectives" canvas — strategic focus area, what the data says, several candidate business objectives each with why and why not — so the decision to start a project rests on facts, and the chosen objective feeds canvas 01. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-strategic-business-objectives))
- **LSC 26 — Success Is a Process Canvas (Management Track)**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Success is a Process" canvas — phase-specific success criteria, weekly current status, learnings and assumptions, and actions — so management does not judge an early release by revenue alone and the metrics change as the service matures. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-success-is-a-process))
- **LSC 27 — MVP Backlog Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "MVP Backlog" canvas — transfer the burning technical issue, business question and end-user verification from the experiment board, then list every action needed to get the MVP out (not only tech tasks) as an owned, limited-WIP backlog. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-mvp-backlog))
- **LSC 28 — What to Measure Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "What to Measure" canvas — define value-proposition metrics, service metrics and business metrics for the MVP so each ties to the problem worth solving, the engagement cycle, and the original business objective. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-what-to-measure))
- **LSC 29 — Growth Hacking Canvas**: Run the Lean Service Creation "Growth Hacking" canvas after launch — visualise the analytics funnel with conversion and volumes, find the reasons behind drop-offs, pick the single most critical blocker, and turn the fix into a measured experiment. ([pantheonzeus/charness](https://github.com/pantheonzeus/charness/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/business-design/lsc-growth-hacking))

Use when the user wants to copy, promote, migrate, sync, or replicate LSC configuration from one org (e.g. a sandbox) to another (e.g. UAT or production), compare config drift, or apply a saved config snapshot. This is for CONFIGURATION metadata, not data records — for loading accounts/HCPs/territory data use lsc-data-migration instead. ([SalesforceLabs/lsc-for-claude](https://github.com/SalesforceLabs/lsc-for-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/lsc-config-migration) · ★10)
